Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the organic_chemistry space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 337 and the convergence rate is O(478/n^1).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 337 + O(1/n^478). Summing over all modes and applying the organic_chemistry convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
